# Changelog

All notable changes to the BPM Platform are documented here.

## [Unreleased] — 2026-08-19

### feat(agt): Stage 17 — AGT-05/AGT-06/AGT-07 TESTED (WF02-agt05-07-20260819)

- **feat(agt): AGT-05 Non-zero RNG seed folded into spec identity TESTED.** `rng_seed` is a mandatory uint64 field on task specs; zero or absent seed returns HTTP 400 `rng_seed_zero` (no default substitution). The seed is folded into the canonical JSON before `spec_hash` is computed, so two specs differing only in seed carry different identities. An artifact submitted with a `spec_hash` computed without the seed returns HTTP 409 `spec_hash_mismatch`. All six ACs verified: zero-seed rejection, absent-seed rejection, seed-differentiated hashes, mismatch detection, seed persistence, canonical serialisation stability. 5/5 PASS (TC-AGT05-01..05). Implemented in `src/api/routes/agent_task_specs.zig`.
- **feat(agt): AGT-06 Dual-sweep artifact retention TESTED.** Retention runs two independent sweeps. Sweep 1 deletes `needs_review` artifacts older than `STAGING_REVIEW_TTL_DAYS` (default 30); sweep 2 deletes `verified` artifacts only when their pinned `(task_spec_version, process_definition_version)` pair has been collected AND `verified_at` is older than `STAGING_VERIFIED_TTL_DAYS` (default 365). On transition to `verified`, an `artifact_version_pins` row is written in the same transaction. Both sweeps run on a daily schedule at 03:00 UTC and are idempotent. All seven ACs verified: needs-review >30d deleted; verified exempt from sweep 1; uncollected pin retained; collected pin <365d retained; collected pin >365d deleted; pin written atomically; sweep idempotency. 7/7 PASS (TC-AGT06-01..07). Implemented in `src/scheduler/artifact_retention.zig`, `src/api/routes/agent_artifacts.zig`; migration `migrations/1173_agt06_artifact_retention.sql`.
- **feat(agt): AGT-07 Deprecated envelope field names rejected TESTED.** The legacy field name `ignore_fields` is rejected with HTTP 400 `deprecated_field:ignore_fields` before any payload schema validation; the response body names the replacement `non_deterministic_fields`. The check fires even when `non_deterministic_fields` is also present, and for empty arrays. No `agent_artifacts` row is written on rejection. All five ACs verified: deprecated-field rejection; combined-field rejection; deprecated check precedes schema validation; no row written; replacement name in error body. 5/5 PASS (TC-AGT07-01..05). Implemented in `src/api/routes/agent_artifacts.zig`.
- **Gate results (run WF02-agt05-07-20260819, branch `feature/WF02-agt05-07-20260819`).** 17/17 PASS: AGT-05 5/5, AGT-06 7/7, AGT-07 5/5. Unit tests PASS. NFR-01 p99 read 2.867 ms (≤200 ms PASS), NFR-01 p99 write 23.355 ms (≤500 ms PASS), NFR-02 append throughput 2359 eps (≥1000 PASS), NFR-04 replay 10 000 events 44.280 ms (≤5000 ms PASS). Release decision `docs/status/release-stage17-agt05-07-20260819.yaml` APPROVED. Requirements AGT-05, AGT-06, AGT-07 advanced to TESTED.

### feat(agt): Stage 18 — AGT-01/AGT-02/AGT-03/AGT-04 TESTED (WF02-agt01-04-20260818)

- **feat(agt): AGT-01 Agent artifact envelope with kind discrimination TESTED.** Artifact submissions now carry a typed envelope with a mandatory `kind` field; the handler dispatches on `kind` before persistence and rejects unknown kinds with HTTP 400 `artifact_kind_unknown`. All six ACs verified: envelope schema validation, kind discrimination routing, unknown-kind rejection, per-kind storage path, audit event `ArtifactSubmitted` with `kind` field, cross-tenant isolation. 6/6 PASS (TC-AGT-01-01..06).
- **feat(agt): AGT-02 Non-production artifact environment enforcement TESTED.** Artifacts tagged with a non-production environment label are rejected at the API boundary when the tenant is operating in production mode; HTTP 403 `artifact_env_mismatch` is returned and no row is persisted. Enforcement runs before transaction begin. All six ACs verified: production-mode rejection, non-production-mode acceptance, environment label validation, pre-transaction enforcement, `ArtifactEnvRejected` audit event, per-tenant mode isolation. 6/6 PASS (TC-AGT-02-01..06).
- **feat(agt): AGT-03 Artifact submission idempotency per attempt TESTED.** Re-submission of an artifact with the same `attempt_id` within an attempt window returns HTTP 200 with the original artifact record (no duplicate row created). Conflicting re-submissions (same `attempt_id`, different body) return HTTP 409 `artifact_attempt_conflict`. All six ACs verified: idempotent re-submit, conflict detection, attempt-window expiry, `ArtifactDuplicateAttempt` audit event, cross-attempt isolation, per-tenant isolation. 6/6 PASS (TC-AGT-03-01..06).
- **feat(agt): AGT-04 Immutable task specs bound by spec hash TESTED.** Task specs are content-addressed: the server computes a canonical JSON hash (`spec_hash`) on submission and stores it immutably. Any attempt to mutate a submitted spec returns HTTP 409 `spec_immutable`. The `spec_hash` is included in every artifact envelope referencing the spec, enabling tamper detection. All six ACs verified: hash determinism, immutability enforcement, hash propagation to artifacts, canonical-JSON stability across key ordering, `TaskSpecMutationRejected` audit event, per-tenant isolation. 6/6 PASS (TC-AGT-04-01..06).
- **Gate results (run WF02-agt01-04-20260818, branch `feature/WF02-agt01-04-20260818`, rework 1).** 24/24 PASS: AGT-01 6/6, AGT-02 6/6, AGT-03 6/6, AGT-04 6/6. Unit tests PASS (359 test-bearing files, no regressions). NFR-01 p99 read 1.043 ms (≤200 ms PASS), NFR-01 p99 write 5.100 ms (≤500 ms PASS), NFR-02 append throughput 50404 eps (≥1000 PASS), NFR-04 replay 10 000 events 49.372 ms (≤5000 ms PASS). Release decision `docs/status/release-agt01-04-20260818.json` APPROVED. Requirements AGT-01, AGT-02, AGT-03, AGT-04 advanced to TESTED.

### feat(sbx): Stage 17 — SBX-04/SBX-05/SBX-06 TESTED (WF02-sbx04-06-20260819)

- **feat(sbx): SBX-04 Sandbox ownership binding at claim TESTED.** A claim binds the sandbox to the triple `(tenant_id, agent_principal, task_spec_id)` under the unique index `ux_sandbox_owner`. Concurrent claims are serialised by the index — exactly one binding is admitted and the loser gets HTTP 409 `sandbox_already_claimed` without disclosing the winner's identity. A released sandbox becomes claimable again; the prior owner's access is cleared. All seven ACs verified: unowned claim → 201 + binding row; nonexistent `task_spec_id` → 404; second principal → 409 no-disclosure; same spec second sandbox → 409; non-owner operation → 403 sentinel; concurrent claim → exactly one admitted; released sandbox → new binding. Implemented in `src/api/routes/agent_sandboxes.zig`, `migrations/1172_sbx04_06_owner_binding.sql`. 7/7 PASS (sbx04_01..07).
- **feat(sbx): SBX-05 Single sentinel for inaccessible sandboxes TESTED.** Nonexistent `sandbox_id`, cross-tenant `sandbox_id`, and wrong-principal access in the same tenant all return HTTP 403 `sandbox_not_accessible` with a byte-identical response body and header set. The tenant predicate is part of the loading query, so unknown and cross-tenant cases execute the same statement shape. After 20 sentinel responses in one minute the principal receives HTTP 429 `probe_rate_exceeded`. All five ACs verified: nonexistent → 403 sentinel; cross-tenant → byte-identical; wrong-principal → same 403; rate limit fires at 21st probe (429); unknown UUID sentinel, no 409. Implemented in `src/api/routes/sandbox_access.zig`, `src/api/routes/agent_sandboxes.zig`. 5/5 PASS (sbx05_01..05).
- **feat(sbx): SBX-06 Sandbox release, reclaim, and claim audit TESTED.** Only the bound `agent_principal` may release a sandbox; non-owner and orchestrator callers both receive HTTP 403 `sandbox_not_accessible`. An idle sandbox (no request for 60 min) is reclaimed by the pool manager, which clears the binding and appends `SandboxReclaimed` naming the prior owner. Every claim, rejected claim, release, and reclaim appends an audit entry carrying `principal`, `tenant_id`, `sandbox_id`, and `task_spec_id`. All five ACs verified: non-owner release → 403 unchanged; orchestrator release → 403; owner release → 204 + `SandboxReleased`; 61-min idle → pool reclaim + `SandboxReclaimed`; 30 sentinel probes → 30 `SandboxClaimRejected` audit entries. Implemented in `src/definition/sandbox_pool.zig`, `src/api/routes/agent_sandboxes.zig`. 5/5 PASS (sbx06_01..05).
- **Gate results (run WF02-sbx04-06-20260819, branch `feature/WF02-sbx04-06-20260819`, rework 2).** 17/17 SBX PASS: SBX-04 7/7, SBX-05 5/5, SBX-06 5/5. Full integration suite 40/40 (including QRY-05 + SBX-01/02/03 regressions). Unit tests PASS (1176/1176, 128 DB-gated skips expected). NFR-01 p99 read 0.771 ms (≤200 ms PASS). Test report `tests/reports/WF02-sbx04-06-20260819-test-report-rework2.yaml`. Release decision `docs/status/release-stage17-sbx04-06-20260819.yaml` APPROVED. Requirements SBX-04, SBX-05, SBX-06 advanced to TESTED.

## [Unreleased] — 2026-08-18

### feat(qry/sbx): Stage 17 — QRY-05/SBX-01-03 TESTED (WF02-qry05-sbx01-03-20260818)

- **feat(qry): QRY-05 Server-side field stripping on entity query results TESTED.** Fields the caller has no read grant for are stripped server-side before serialisation — absent from the JSON object, not nulled or redacted. Ungranted fields used as filter or sort terms return HTTP 400 `filter_field_not_allowlisted`. Items stripped to `record_id` only are still present; row visibility and field visibility are independent. Cross-tenant grant isolation verified. Implemented in `src/entities/query/field_grants.zig`, `src/entities/query/allowlist.zig`, `src/api/routes/entity_query.zig`; migration `migrations/1169_qry05_entity_field_grants.sql`.
- **feat(sbx): SBX-01 Orchestrator role gate on task-spec submission TESTED.** `POST /api/v1/agent/task-specs` now requires both the `agent.submit_task_spec` scope and the `tenant_orchestrator` realm role; either credential alone returns HTTP 403 `orchestrator_role_required`. The gate runs in the request handler (not a gateway rule), so a direct service-port call is subject to the same check. A principal holding both `tenant_orchestrator` and `tenant_implementer` is rejected with HTTP 403 `conflicting_agent_roles`. Every rejected submission appends `TaskSpecSubmissionRejected`. Implemented in `src/api/middleware/agent_auth.zig`, `src/api/routes/agent_task_specs.zig`; migration `migrations/1170_sbx_task_specs_agent_sandboxes.sql`.
- **feat(sbx): SBX-02 Server-authoritative orchestrator principal TESTED.** The task-spec handler sets `orchestrator_principal` to the verified token subject before canonicalisation and discards any client-supplied value without error. The AGT-04 `spec_hash` therefore covers the server-set principal; two orchestrators submitting byte-identical bodies produce different canonical forms and different hashes. Implemented in `src/crypto/canonical_json.zig`, `src/api/routes/agent_task_specs.zig`.
- **feat(sbx): SBX-03 Orchestrator excluded from sandbox claim; supervisory read TESTED.** The sandbox claim endpoint rejects `tenant_orchestrator` callers with HTTP 403 `orchestrator_may_not_claim` and requires `tenant_implementer`, returning HTTP 403 `implementer_role_required` otherwise. Orchestrators retain supervisory read via `GET /api/v1/agent/sandboxes` (state, owner principal, `task_spec_id` for every tenant sandbox; no execution endpoint). Implementers see only their own sandboxes. Every rejected claim appends `SandboxClaimRejected`. Implemented in `src/api/routes/agent_sandboxes.zig`; migration `migrations/1170_sbx_task_specs_agent_sandboxes.sql`.
- **Gate results (run WF02-qry05-sbx01-03-20260818, branch `feature/WF02-qry05-sbx01-03-20260818`, rework 1).** 23/23 PASS: QRY-05 6/6, SBX-01 6/6, SBX-02 5/5, SBX-03 6/6. Test report `tests/reports/WF02-qry05-sbx01-03-20260818-test-report-rework1.yaml`. Release decision `docs/status/release-stage17-qry05-sbx01-03-20260818.yaml` APPROVED. Known gap: `sec_minor_01` — conflicting-roles audit path not yet emitting `TaskSpecSubmissionRejected` (non-blocking, tracked separately). Requirements QRY-05, SBX-01, SBX-02, SBX-03 advanced to TESTED.

### fix(qry): WF-03 bugs — QRY-01/02/03/04 TESTED (WF03-qry01-04-bugs-20260818)

- **fix(qry): GH-823 — allowlist typed-column merge now works correctly.** The entity query compiler now correctly merges typed-column names with `filterable_jsonb_keys` entries; previously a typed column could bypass the allowlist check when a JSONB key of the same name existed, producing either a double-predicate or a silent miss. Fix in `src/api/routes/entity_query.zig`. Test TC-QRY-02-06 (typed-column vs JSONB-key shadowing).
- **fix(qry): GH-824 — error field name corrected.** HTTP 400 responses for allowlist violations now emit `"field"` (not `"column"`) matching the VLD-03 Problem Details schema. Fix in `src/api/routes/entity_query.zig`.
- **fix(qry): GH-825 — cursor encoding/decoding fixed.** Base64url cursor encoding no longer emits standard Base64 padding; decoding validates the strict base64url alphabet and rejects malformed cursors with HTTP 400 `cursor_malformed`. Fix in `src/query/cursor.zig`. Test TC-QRY-03-06.
- **fix(qry): GH-826 — test audit column reference corrected.** Integration test fixture referenced `audit_col` instead of the canonical `audit_entry` column in `entity_definitions`; updated to match the migration-defined schema. Fix in `tests/integration/qry01_04_entity_query_test.zig`.
- **Additional inline fixes (entity_definitions schema and cursor UAF).** `entity_definitions` query now uses per-tenant schema prefix (removes `public.` qualifier that broke fresh-provisioned schemas). Cursor decode freed its allocation before the query ran (UAF); fixed to defer free until after query completion. Payload column fixture corrected; HTTP 422 detail field populated. Commits: `3ced4800`, `c0459571`, `db2bd848`, `2c4ed368`.
- **Gate results (run WF03-qry01-04-bugs-20260818, branch `feature/WF02-qry01-04-20260818`).** 14/14 PASS (QRY-01 5/5, QRY-02 3/3, QRY-03 4/4, QRY-04 2/2). GitHub issues #823, #824, #825, #826 closed. Requirements QRY-01, QRY-02, QRY-03, QRY-04 at TESTED.

## [Unreleased] — 2026-08-17

### feat(obp/ddl): Stage 16 — OBP-01/02/03 DDL-03 RELEASED (WF02-obp-ddl-20260817)

- **feat(obp): OBP-01 Outbox depth cap and cached depth counter RELEASED.** The outbox now enforces a per-tenant depth cap (`BPM_OUTBOX_DEPTH_CAP`, default 50000). Depth is read from a lockless cached counter refreshed by the drainer every 250 ms; no `SELECT count(*)` is issued on the request path. A counter older than 5 s is treated as at-cap (closed rather than open on visibility loss). All four ACs verified: drainer refresh interval (AC1), sub-1 ms request overhead / no count(*) (AC2), stale-counter → at-cap behavior (AC3), per-tenant isolation (AC4). `BPM_OUTBOX_DEPTH_CAP` and `BPM_OUTBOX_LOW_WATER` documented in `.env.example`. Implemented in `src/outbox/depth.zig`, `src/effects/worker.zig`.
- **feat(obp): OBP-02 External ingress refusal before transaction RELEASED.** OBP-02 middleware (`src/api/middleware/outbox_cap.zig`) reads the cached depth counter and returns HTTP 429 with `Retry-After: 5` and body `{"error":"outbox_at_capacity","depth":<n>,"cap":<n>}` before `BEGIN` is issued, before a pool connection is taken, and before the idempotency key is recorded. All six ACs verified: no `plat_idempotency_key` row on refusal (AC1), retry treated as first attempt after gate reopens (AC2), no transaction/connection on refused path (AC3), 429 and never 400/500/503 (AC4), `Retry-After` unconditional (AC5), `EXECUTION_INGRESS_REFUSED` audit event appended (AC6). Migration `migrations/1167_obp01_plat_outbox_gate_depth_ts.sql`.
- **feat(obp): OBP-03 Typed outbox overflow on internal emit RELEASED.** `src/outbox/emit.zig` returns `OutboxOverflow` (a typed error) instead of silently dropping or committing when the cached depth is at or above the cap. The calling step transaction rolls back in full; no partial DB state results. Implemented in `src/outbox/emit.zig`, `src/effects/mod.zig`.
- **feat(ddl): DDL-03 Phased DDL generation RELEASED.** `src/platform/ddl_generate.zig` generates three-phase DDL (expand, backfill, constrain) for safe zero-downtime schema changes, satisfying the DDL-01/DDL-02 gate requirements that reject unbounded exclusive-lock operations. Implemented in `src/platform/ddl_generate.zig`.
- **Gate results (run WF02-obp-ddl-20260817, branch `feature/WF02-obp-ddl-20260817`).** `zig build` PASS (exit 0); unit 1176/1240 (0 new failures; 64 pre-existing skips; 2 pre-existing binary exits: iss0173-orphan, ISS-602-differential-corpus — unrelated to OBP files); test-integration-obp01-depth 3/3; test-integration-obp02 4/4; test-integration-obp03 6/6. Rework 2 resolved 4 test logic bugs (ISS-OBP-T01 dangling stale-flag re-query; ISS-OBP-T02 dangling pointer in TestResponseWriter; ISS-OBP-T03 leaked URL allocation; ISS-OBP-T04 OBP-03 logic error). Step-05 RELEASE-VALIDATOR APPROVED (decision `docs/status/release-stage16-WF02-obp-ddl-20260817.yaml`) — NFR-01 PASS (analytical: lockless atomic read adds < 1 ms; refused requests return before BEGIN), NFR-07 PASS (no partial writes on refusal or overflow path). Minor security follow-ups: SEC-MINOR-1 (ddl_generate.zig comment over-claims identifier safety for backfill_expr/column_type — non-blocking, operator-authored fields), SEC-MINOR-2 (migration 1167 missing `INSERT INTO event_type_registry` for `EXECUTION_INGRESS_REFUSED` — audit append best-effort, core refusal behavior correct). Requirements OBP-01, OBP-02, OBP-03, DDL-03 advanced to RELEASED.

### fix(ddl): GH-814/ISS-0716 — DDL-04 plat_migration_state bookkeeping RESOLVED (WF03-GH814-20260817)

- **fix(ddl): GH-814/ISS-0716 — DDL-04 plat_migration_state bookkeeping RESOLVED.** `recordBatchProgress` in `src/platform/backfill.zig` now accepts the current adaptive `batch_size` as a parameter and persists it to the `backfill_batch_size` column (replacing the hardcoded literal `5000`). A new `recordTerminalStatus` call is made after the backfill loop exits, writing status `applied` or `failed` (based on the stall flag) along with the final adaptive `backfill_batch_size` to `plat_migration_state` — closing the gap where the persisted row was permanently left with `status = 'running'`. New integration test `TC-DDL-04-AC6b` in `tests/integration/ddl04_backfill_loop_test.zig` asserts the terminal status and adaptive batch_size values in `plat_migration_state` at loop completion. Test spec `tests/specs/DDL-04.md` updated. Gate results: ddl04 7/7 (incl TC-DDL-04-AC6b 1250 adaptive), unit 0 fail, `zig build` exit 0. Commits `9387094c`, `07a7deab`, branch `feature/WF03-GH814-20260817`. GitHub issue #814 remains open pending Step Final.

### fix(vld): GH-816/ISS-0717 — VLD-04 live PATCH draft-save surface gate enforcement RESOLVED (WF03-GH816-20260817)

- **fix(vld): GH-816/ISS-0717 — VLD-04 AC1 PATCH gate wiring RESOLVED.** `handlePatch` in `src/api/routes/definitions.zig` (the live `PATCH /api/v1/definitions/{id}` draft-save handler, the only surface the frontend uses via `client.patch`) now invokes `runSemanticGate` and returns HTTP 422 with the VLD-03 Problem Details body on findings — mirroring the gate block already present in `handlePut`. The gap existed because `handlePut` (dead code — no PUT route is registered in `src/main.zig`) was the only gate-wired handler; `handlePatch` called `store.update` and returned HTTP 200 unconditionally. Fix: gate block copied from `handlePut` (lines 412-444) into `handlePatch`; `src/bpm.zig` updated to expose `runSemanticGate` to `handlePatch`. New handler-level integration test `TC-VLD-04-AC1-PATCH` in `tests/integration/vld04_gate_test.zig` asserts HTTP 422 on a finding-producing definition saved via `PATCH`. Test spec `tests/specs/VLD-04.md` updated. Gate results: vld04 8/8 (incl TC-VLD-04-AC1-PATCH), unit 1155/1219 0 fail, `zig build` exit 0. Commit `b9f61314`, branch `feature/WF03-GH816-20260817`. GitHub issue #816 remains open pending Step Final.

## [Unreleased] — 2026-08-16

### feat(ddl/obp/ord/vld): Stage 16 batch 7 — DDL-04/OBP-04/ORD-03/VLD-04 RELEASED (WF02-batch-7-20260816)

- **feat(ddl): DDL-04 Idempotent batched backfill RELEASED.** The DDL generator's backfill loop (`src/platform/backfill.zig`) is now idempotent and batched: it runs in per-batch committed transactions with the `IS NULL` predicate as the resume source of truth (AC1), rejects non-idempotent / non-batched backfills (AC2), commits each batch before the next begins with no outer transaction (AC3), adaptively halves the batch size with a 500-row floor / 50000 upper bound (AC4), escalates a stalled run to `failed` carrying `rows_remaining` (AC5), and records durable per-batch progress (`rows_updated_total`, `rows_remaining`, `last_batch_rows`, `last_batch_ms`, `stall_count`) on the same connection/transaction as each batch commit (AC6). Migration `migrations/1163_ddl04_plat_migration_state.sql` adds `plat_migration_state`, one row per `(migration_id, tenant_schema, phase)` with status `pending -> running -> applied | failed`. 2 MINOR bookkeeping follow-ups tracked as ISS-0716/GH-814 (non-blocking).
- **feat(obp): OBP-04 Outbox gate hysteresis and escalation RELEASED.** The outbox ingress gate (`src/outbox/gate.zig`) stays closed across 49999/50001 depth oscillation and reopens only at the 80% low-water mark (AC1 hysteresis), appends exactly one `EXECUTION_OUTBOX_GATE_OPENED` event carrying the closed duration in the same transaction as the state transition (AC2), restores the low-water mark on flapping recovery (AC3), escalates on refusal-threshold crossing via an alert hook with severity `escalate` (AC4), pages on excessive closed duration with the drainer-restart operator action (AC5), and is keyed per tenant schema so one tenant's depth never refuses another tenant's ingress (AC6). Migration `migrations/1164_obp04_plat_outbox_gate.sql` adds `plat_outbox_gate` (state, depth, cap, low_water, closed_duration_ms, refusal counters) with a partial closed-gate index.
- **feat(ord): ORD-03 Sequence order guard and gap sweeping RELEASED.** `src/ordering/cursor.zig`/`consumer.zig`/`sweeper.zig` defer out-of-order sequences with a silent ROLLBACK leaving the row PENDING (AC1), apply ordered sequences with a cursor advance and ordered `EXECUTION_EFFECT_APPLIED` events (AC2), guard double-apply via conditional cursor advance (AC3), roll back on apply failure with no cursor/row divergence (AC4), sweep stalled gaps to DEAD with a `+ 1` predicate that never dead-letters a slow-but-present correlation (AC5), and absorb re-inserts via `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` (AC6). Migration `migrations/1166_ord03_ordering_event_types_seed.sql` registers `EXECUTION_EFFECT_APPLIED` (plus ORD-04's `EXECUTION_CORRELATION_LAG`/`EXECUTION_CORRELATION_CONTENTION`) in `event_type_registry` with `retain_forever`, following the 1152 pattern. Module-level release, consistent with ORD-01/02/04 precedent.
- **feat(vld): VLD-04 Validation gate at authoring and promotion RELEASED.** `src/validation/gate.zig` runs a semantic gate at draft-save (`handlePut`), `POST /api/v1/definitions/{id}/validate`, and both promotion-submit surfaces (`handleCreatePromotionPlan` + `handleSubmitPromotion`), mapping `.invalid`/`.timeout` to HTTP 422 with the VLD-03 Problem Details body and no `promotion_reviews` row (AC1/AC2), re-runs when the stored verdict is stale (`storedVerdictIsCurrent` false, AC3), enforces a compile-time budget (AC4), and appends `DEFINITION_VALIDATED` / `DEFINITION_VALIDATION_FAILED` events carrying the finding count (AC5). Migration `migrations/1165_vld04_definition_semantic_verdict.sql` adds the `semantically_valid`, `compiler_version`, `validated_at` and `validation_finding_count` verdict columns to `process_definitions`, and seeds the two DEFINITION_* verdict event types. ISS-0715/GH-813 (the AC1/AC2 handler-wiring gap) resolved end-to-end via REWORK-2 (commits `da4761fc` + `8cdde77e`); VLD-04 moved TESTED -> RELEASED.

### feat(spt): Stage SPT — SPT-02/03/04 Schema-Per-Tenant Migration RELEASED (WF02-spt02-04-20260816)

- **feat(spt): SPT-02 Data migration — copy rows into tenant schemas and remove RLS RELEASED.** For every distinct `tenant_id` the platform calls `bpm_provision_tenant_schema()` (schema created if absent), copies all of that tenant's rows from every public table into the tenant schema in a single atomic transaction, then drops the `tenant_id` columns, the `bpm_effective_tenant_id()` function, all RLS policies, and all tenant-scoped composite indexes from public. Implemented by migrations **061** `061_data_copy_into_tenant_schemas.sql`, **062** `062_remove_tenant_id_rls_from_public.sql`, **063** `063_rls_policy_idempotency_recheck.sql`. Interrupted copies are detected via `public.tenant_schemas.data_migrated_at IS NULL` and 062's pre-flight gates re-issue `DROP POLICY IF EXISTS` for every known policy (belt-and-suspenders idempotency); re-running 061–063 is a no-op that leaves state unchanged.

Migration 1163 `1163_events_ephemeral_tenant_id_default.sql` gives `events` an ephemeral tenant_id default so the per-tenant event log remains consistent under the new routing.

### feat(vld): Stage 16 — VLD-01/02/03 RELEASED (WF03-vld-impl-bugs-20260816)

- **feat(vld): VLD-01 Typed environment from definition context RELEASED.** The platform builds a typed environment for expression validation from the definition's own declarations only: the `variable_schema`, the declared result schemas of referenced service catalog entries (REPO-07, SVC-01), the declared output schemas of referenced `module_ref` modules (PLC-01), and the field types of each human task's form schema. Type names map (`string`/`text`/`enum`→string, `integer`/`decimal`/`money`→number, `boolean`→bool, `date`/`datetime`→timestamp, `list<T>`→list, `object`→map). A type name outside the mapping returns HTTP 422 `UnknownVariableType` naming the variable and the type; a SERVICE_TASK referencing a catalog entry with no result schema returns HTTP 422 `UndeclaredResultSchema`; two same-name form fields with different types in one human-task scope return HTTP 422 `ConflictingFieldType`. No instance variable value contributes a type; a node output is visible only to forward-reachable sites and a form field type only inside its own human-task scope.
- **feat(vld): VLD-02 Expression compile and type check RELEASED.** The platform compiles every CEL expression carried by a definition against the VLD-01 typed environment visible at its site (transition guards, human-task assignment expressions, timer delay expressions, service-task input mappings, form `visible_when`/`computed_from`), running only after the PD-02 structure check and the PD-06 syntax check pass. Required result types: bool for a guard, duration or timestamp for a timer delay, and the declared field type for `computed_from`. A guard compiling to a non-bool type records `TypeMismatch` naming bool and the actual type; an identifier absent from the environment records `UnknownVariable`; a string+number operand pair records `OperandTypeError` naming the operator and both operand types (operand-compat check added to the `.cmp_expr` branch of `src/validation/typecheck.zig` for ISS-0709 B1); a PD-06 syntax failure short-circuits before semantic compilation with PD-06 diagnostics only; an empty or whitespace-only site records `EmptyExpression` rather than passing.
- **feat(vld): VLD-03 Aggregated validation diagnostics RELEASED.** A single validation pass reports every semantic finding in one HTTP 422 response — validation does not stop at the first failure. Each finding carries `node_id`, `expression_path`, `source`, `error_kind` and `message`; `error_kind` is a closed enumeration of exactly `UnknownVariable`, `TypeMismatch`, `OperandTypeError`, `UnknownVariableType`, `UndeclaredResultSchema`, `ConflictingFieldType`, `EmptyExpression` — no other value is emitted. An `UnknownVariable` message names the referenced identifier and the nearest declared identifier by edit distance. Findings are ordered by `node_id` then `expression_path`, so two validations of the same definition produce identical ordering.
- **Gate results (run WF03-vld-impl-bugs-20260816, branch `feature/wf02-vld01-03-20260816`; ISS-0709/GH #799 verified end-to-end).** `zig build` exit 0; test-env-verify 10/10; test-validation 40/40; test-integration-vld-unit 14/14; test-integration-vld-http 5/5 (0 leak tokens, 0 42P01); full corpus 0 fail/0 crash attributable to this run (3 pre-existing definition-store/engine build-wiring defects documented as unrelated). ISS-0709 fix commits `844c2016` (B1 operand-compat) + `1c3a6065` (B2/B3 literal-only fixture + cross-tenant provisioning). Implementation: `src/validation/{mod,env,scope,typecheck,site,pd06,finding,wire}.zig`; test specs `tests/specs/vld-01-03-stage-16-validation.spec.md`; test coverage `tests/integration/validation_vld_unit_test.zig` + `tests/integration/validation_vld_http_test.zig`. ### fix(prod+test): PRM-09 public-schema qualification fix — GH-792/ISS-0707 (RESOLVED)

- **fix(prod+test): GH-792/ISS-0707 — PRM-09 solution-pack update public-schema qualification RESOLVED.** Both the SUT (`src/definition/pack_update.zig`, **production**) and its integration test (`tests/integration/prm-09-pack-update.test.zig`) referenced the PRM-09 global tables `solution_pack_installs` and `solution_pack_artefact_bases` **unqualified**, relying on `search_path` fallback. Under the pool's SCHEMA-mode routing the search path is `tenant_default, public`, so an unqualified name resolves to `tenant_default.solution_pack_installs` first — the per-tenant shadow created by migration **1158** (SOL-02, `-- scope: tenant_only`) with a different column layout (`pack_id, pack_version, schema_version, installed_by, installed_at` — no `tenant_id`/`installed_version`) — raising sqlstate 42703 on every PRM-09 statement against a fresh-migration database. The intended destination is `public.solution_pack_installs` / `public.solution_pack_artefact_bases`, created by migration **1157 which is public-scoped (`-- scope: public.`)** (the `tenant_default` shadow is 1158, not 1157 — the earlier record's "tenant_only" prose was stale). Fixed per `src/design/iss0707-gh792-prm09-public-qualify-fix.md` by explicitly `public.`-qualifying every DML/SELECT reference to the two global tables in **both** files: test file L98 `INSERT INTO public.solution_pack_installs`, L119 `SELECT id::text FROM public.solution_pack_installs`, L128 `INSERT INTO public.solution_pack_artefact_bases`, L164 `DELETE FROM public.solution_pack_installs`; production `pack_update.zig` L120 `FROM public.solution_pack_installs` (Step 1 Vb load) and L182 `FROM public.solution_pack_artefact_bases` (Step 2 base load). The doc comment at `pack_update.zig` L102–107 was updated to state the explicit `public.` qualification (no longer search-path fallback). The SUT itself issued unqualified queries that would 42703 on a fresh DB, so this was not a test-only defect. `process_definitions` stays **tenant-scoped and unqualified** (test L145/L160, SUT L238): it is a per-tenant business table reached through the per-tenant search path; qualifying it with `public.` would route the Step 3 `theirs` lookup to a non-existent relation. This fix touches exactly two files and no migrations.

### fix(prod): rollback.zig Step 5 supersede UPDATE — GH-803/ISS-0712 (RESOLVED)

- **fix(prod): GH-803/ISS-0712 — production rollback supersede fix RESOLVED.** `src/definition/rollback.zig` Step 5 supersede UPDATE compared `promotion_reviews.def_id` (TEXT, migration 096) against `$3::uuid` — no implicit `text = uuid` operator, so PostgreSQL raised sqlstate 42883 ('operator does not exist') at execution time; the bare `catch null` wrapper (written to tolerate the table not existing when PRM-04 is a later batch) swallowed the real error, so `superseded_review_id` was ALWAYS null and PRM-08 AC4 (rollback supersedes a matching `promotion_reviews` row) never worked. Two-part production fix (per `src/design/iss0712-gh803-rollback-supersede-fix.md`): **Change A** — line 290 `AND def_id = $3::uuid` → `AND def_id = $3` (drop the `::uuid` cast; `current_active_id` is already a uuid string bound as text, so PostgreSQL infers text = text against the TEXT column); `$1::uuid`/`$2::uuid` unchanged (uuid columns). **Change B** — line 295 bare `catch null` replaced with a SQLSTATE-distinguishing catch mirroring the PAR-01 pattern in `src/event_store/store.zig`: copy `conn.lastSqlState()` into a 5-byte local buffer; SQLSTATE `42P01` (genuinely missing table) → `null` (PRM-04 later batch); everything else mapped via `switch` (`PoolError.ExhaustedPool` → `RollbackError.PoolExhausted`, else → `RollbackError.TransactionFailed`) so real query failures surface as `RollbackError` instead of a swallowed 200 with null `superseded_review_id`. No migration needed (schema is correct; the bug was the query cast).
- Verification: Step-05 TEST-RUNNER independent PASS — `BPM_TEST_DB_URL=postgres://bpm:bpm@localhost:5453/bpm_test zig build test-integration-prm08` (direct, NOT the stale-5433 wrapper) exit 0, **4/4 green** (TC-PRM-08-01..04 including the previously-failing TC-PRM-08-03 supersede — `superseded_review_id` now populated), **no sqlstate=42883** in stderr (direct binary run: "All 4 tests passed"); change-scope audit confirms the production change is confined to `src/definition/rollback.zig` (no migrations/, no tests/ changes); `zig build test-env-verify` HEALTHY 10/10; `zig build test` exit 0 (unit suite, `lint_test_wiring` PASS). `python tools/lint_handoffs.py` exit 0 (0 BLOCKER, 0 MAJOR, 1 pre-existing MINOR H010, 332 ACK). ### fix(process): ORCH dispatch-time staleness gate — GH-797/ISS-0710 (RESOLVED)

- **fix(process): GH-797/ISS-0710 — ORCH dispatch-time staleness gate RESOLVED.** An ADHOC run dispatched against an outdated view of the repo can target work that is already complete at `origin/main`. This was observed in `ADHOC-prm-reqctl-status-20260816`: it was dispatched to mark PRM-02/03/04/05 `RELEASED` in `docs/requirements.yaml`, but that status had already been delivered independently by `WF02-prm02-05-20260816` (PR #795). The in-run pre-flight correctly aborted BLOCKER, but only **after** step-00 had already created the housekeeping branch `housekeeping/reqctl-status-stale-20260816` and a setup stash, which were then orphaned. Root cause: no cheap dispatch-time check existed between "ORCH authorizes the ADHOC against a possibly-stale repo view" and "step-00 mutates the repo". Fix (Type E, docs-only): new ORCH construction rule (§5 rule 7) + pre-dispatch gate §8f "Dispatch-Time Staleness Check" in `docs/agents/ORCHESTRATOR.md`, mirrored to the canonical `.claude/agents/orchestrator.md` and both GitHub-harness adapters (`.github/instructions/orchestrator.instructions.md`, `.github/agents/orchestrator.agent.md`) per `AGENT_SYSTEM.md` §9 (A007 — adapters must not drift). The gate runs **before** any `fn:git-setup` dispatch: `git fetch origin`, then reads each target requirement's status at `origin/main` HEAD via `git show origin/main:docs/requirements.yaml` (never the working tree, never the local `main` branch); if any target is already at the ADHOC's declared desired end state, the run short-circuits with a BLOCKER before step-00 creates a branch or stash. Read failure (fetch/show error, missing ID, unparseable status) is fail-closed. Judged by exit code only per §8a/§8e — never phrase an ADHOC task as "make X stop appearing". Logged to `handoffs/orchestrator.log` with action `DISPATCH_STALE` (CLEARED/BLOCKER lines). `reqctl.py show <id>` is a valid substitute only when the working tree is verified to be at `origin/main` HEAD. No production code — no Zig, no SQL, no TypeScript.
- Verification: `tests/reports/report-20260816-WF03-GH797-20260816.yaml` (Step-05 TEST-RUNNER PASS). `lint_handoffs.py` exit 0 (0 BLOCKER, 0 MAJOR; 1 pre-existing MINOR H010); `lint_agent_docs.py` exit 0 with no new A007 drift (baseline updated to reflect improved similarity: orchestrator.agent.md 0.40→0.43, orchestrator.instructions.md 0.42→0.45); §8f heading + rule 7 + `DISPATCH_STALE` log lines verbatim in all four surfaces; `lint_design_artefact.py` OK; no prod-code/migration files changed. Run `WF03-GH797-20260816`, branch `feature/WF03-GH797-20260816`. GitHub issue #797 intentionally left open for Step Final (`release.py --close-github-issue`); Step-Final squash-merge handled by the Step Final dispatch.

## [Unreleased] — 2026-08-15

### feat(prm): Stage 16 — PRM-02/03/04/05 RELEASED (WF02-prm02-05-20260816)

- **feat(prm): PRM-02 Conflict pre-flight rejection RELEASED.** The platform runs conflict detection as the first step of the promotion pipeline, before any transaction opens. A conflict exists when the target tenant's active version of `process_key` is greater than the `base_version` the source branched from. On conflict the platform returns HTTP 409 `PromotionConflict` with a body listing `{definition_id, source_change, target_change}` for each conflict, appends exactly one `DEFINITION_PROMOTION_REJECTED` event in its own transaction, creates no `promotion_reviews` / `promotion_assertion_runs` row, and holds no transaction open against the target tenant schema at the conflict raise. The conflict check executes before the plan digest is computed (PRM-03) and before the review row is inserted (PRM-04); no ordering makes it run later.
- **feat(prm): PRM-03 Plan digest binds approval to a diff RELEASED.** The platform binds every approval to a `plan_digest`: the lowercase hexadecimal SHA-256 over the canonical JSON serialisation of the promotion plan (object keys sorted lexicographically, no insignificant whitespace, entry shape `{type, id, changes}`). The digest and the full serialised plan are stored on the `promotion_reviews` row at submit time; approve and apply both require the digest in the request body and a mismatch is rejected with HTTP 409 `PlanDigestMismatch`. The reviewer context endpoint serves the plan stored at submit time and never re-computes a live diff.
- **feat(prm): PRM-04 Promotion review state machine RELEASED.** The platform persists promotion approvals in a `promotion_reviews` table (`id`, `tenant_id`, `plan_digest`, `def_type`, `def_id`, serialised plan, `status`, `requested_by`, `approved_by`, `approved_at`, `superseded_by`, `row_version`, `created_at`, `updated_at`) with a CHECK over exactly six status values (`pending_review`, `approved`, `rejected`, `applied`, `failed`, `superseded`) and a partial unique index `promotion_reviews_plan_digest_active_uniq` over `(tenant_id, plan_digest) WHERE status IN ('pending_review','approved')` permitting at most one live review per digest. Illegal transitions are rejected by the DB CHECK and returned as HTTP 400 `InvalidReviewTransition`; a second live review for the same digest returns HTTP 409 `DuplicateReview`; a failed assertion re-run moves `approved` → `failed`; apply moves `approved` → `applied` with `DEFINITION_PROMOTION_APPLIED` appended in the same transaction as the version-pointer move.
- **feat(prm): PRM-05 Non-skippable human approval gate RELEASED.** Apply without an approved review returns HTTP 400 `InvalidReviewTransition` and claims no sandbox; self-approval (principal equals `requested_by`) returns HTTP 403 and the review stays `pending_review`; no request parameter, header, flag or configuration value bypasses the check (an unrecognised skip field returns HTTP 422). `GET /api/v1/promotions/{id}/context` returns the serialised plan, `assertions[]`, the `NEEDS_REVIEW` package and `plan_digest` in one document so the reviewer decides on exactly the diff the digest binds.
- **Gate results (run WF02-prm02-05-20260816, branch `feature/WF02-prm02-05-20260816`).** `zig build check` exit 0 (`check_fmt_scope` PASSED); test-integration-prm02 8/8, prm03 11/11, prm04 umbrella 7/7, prm04-review-sm 13/13, prm05-review-gate 13/13, test-integration-tnt 34/34. One branch-introduced BLOCKER (TC-PRM-02-05 advisory-lock self-deadlock: `acquireIntegrationLock` then `ensureSchemaReady` on the same `bpm_test_migrations_public` lock across two connections) was fixed under Unblock-Everything by reordering `ensureSchemaReady()` before `acquireIntegrationLock()`; re-run green with zero remaining BLOCKER/MAJOR regressions. Implementation: `src/definition/promotion_digest.zig`, `src/definition/promotion_review.zig`, `src/definition/promotion_conflict.zig`, `src/api/routes/promotion_review.zig`, `src/bpm.zig`; migration `migrations/096_promotion_reviews.sql` (14 cols, `chk_promotion_reviews_status` CHECK over the six statuses, `promotion_reviews_plan_digest_active_uniq` partial unique index); test specs `tests/specs/PRM-02.md`, `tests/specs/PRM-03.md`, `tests/specs/PRM-04.md`, `tests/specs/PRM-05.md`; test report `tests/reports/report-20260816-WF02-prm02-05-20260816.yaml`. Release-decision file: `docs/status/release-decision-WF02-prm02-05-20260816.yaml` (APPROVED at 2026-08-15T23:23:36Z).

### feat(spc): Stage 15 batch — SPC-01/SPC-02 RELEASED (WF02-plc-batch-b-20260815)

- **feat(spc): SPC-01 Declared input/output contract for SUB_PROCESS nodes RELEASED.** A SUB_PROCESS node may declare an `interface` object with `inputs` (array of `{name, json_schema, required}`) and `outputs` (array of `{name, json_schema, required}`). When declared, only the named input variables are copied into the child instance's initial variable map (each validated against its `json_schema` before the child is created); on child completion only the named output variables are merged into the parent's variable map per EE-09, and any other variable the child produced is discarded. A missing required input, a schema-invalid input, or a missing required output transitions the parent instance to ERROR per EE-10 with a structured reason — no child instance is ever created on an activation-gate failure (atomic, no orphaned child). When `interface` is omitted, EXT-05 behaviour is unchanged (full parent map copied in, full child map merged back). Implementation: `src/definition/sub_process_interface.zig` (interface data model + runtime helpers `buildChildInitialMap`/`selectAndValidateOutputs`), `src/engine/instance.zig` (activation + completion gates, four new `ErrorType` variants `SUB_PROCESS_MISSING_REQUIRED_INPUT`/`SUB_PROCESS_INPUT_SCHEMA_VIOLATION`/`SUB_PROCESS_MISSING_REQUIRED_OUTPUT`/`SUB_PROCESS_OUTPUT_SCHEMA_VIOLATION` emitting structured `EXECUTION_ERROR` payloads with `affected_node`/`affected_field`/constraint pointer), `src/tools/json_schema.zig`, `src/definition/graph.zig`, `build.zig`; unit coverage `tests/unit/graph_node_attributes_test.zig`; integration coverage `tests/integration/spc01_sub_process_interface_test.zig`; test spec `tests/specs/SPC-01.md`. No migration required — the interface lives in the node's attributes JSONB.
- **feat(spc): SPC-02 Contract validated at definition-time RELEASED.** On creation or update of a definition containing a SUB_PROCESS node with a declared `interface`, the platform validates that every `json_schema` under `inputs`/`outputs` is itself a well-formed JSON Schema, rejecting the definition with HTTP 422 otherwise — identifying the offending schema and its node. The `validateSchemaShape` rule in `src/tools/json_schema.zig` (recognised keywords typed, unknown keywords inert, depth cap 32) is enforced in `src/definition/graph.zig` `checkSubProcess` (after PD-02), with `collectInterfaceViolations` returning every offending entry (direction/name/RFC 6901 pointer). Client-side, the SPA performs the same validation before submit via `web/src/utils/canvas/interfaceValidation.ts` (wired through `web/src/components/canvas/PropertyPanel.tsx`, `web/src/components/canvas/ProcessCanvas.tsx`, `web/src/pages/definitions/DefinitionEditorPage.tsx`, `web/src/types/api.ts`). Test spec `tests/specs/SPC-02.md`.

### feat(plc): Stage 15 batch — PLC-01/02/03/04 RELEASED (WF02-plc-batch-a-20260815)

- **feat(plc): PLC-01 Process module catalog RELEASED.** The platform maintains a process module catalog of reusable sub-process definitions with `module_id` (globally unique per publishing tenant), `version` (semver), `owning_definition_id`, `interface_schema`, `exportable` (boolean, default true), and `status` (DRAFT | ACTIVE | DEPRECATED). A SUB_PROCESS node may reference a catalog entry via `module_ref: {module_id, version_constraint}` instead of (or as well as? — see AC) a tenant-local `child_definition_id`. Both forms on the same node is rejected with HTTP 422; neither form is rejected with HTTP 422. Legacy `child_definition_id` references continue to work unchanged. `module_ref` resolution picks the highest ACTIVE version satisfying `version_constraint` visible to the resolving tenant. Implementation: `migrations/1161_plc01_process_module_catalog.sql`, `migrations/1162_plc01_module_id_unique_per_tenant.sql`; `src/repository/process_module_catalog.zig`, `src/api/routes/process_modules.zig`; UI `web/src/pages/admin/modules/ProcessModulesPage.tsx`; integration coverage `tests/integration/plc01_module_catalog_test.zig` (8/8 PASS); test spec `tests/specs/plc-01-04-process-module-catalog.md`.
- **feat(plc): PLC-02 Catalog entry publication requires declared interface RELEASED.** A process module may be published to the catalog (DRAFT → ACTIVE) only if its designated SUB_PROCESS entry point has a fully declared SPC-01 interface. Publication of a module without a declared interface fails with HTTP 422 identifying the missing interface. Publication by an authorised caller (PROCESS_DESIGNER or above) transitions the module to ACTIVE and makes it resolvable via `module_ref`. Schema: `migrations/1161_plc01_process_module_catalog.sql` (interface column); implementation `src/repository/process_module_catalog.zig` (publish path); integration coverage `tests/integration/plc02_publication_test.zig` (6/6 PASS).
- **feat(plc): PLC-03 Cross-version compatibility check on publish RELEASED.** When a new version of an already-cataloged module is published, the platform compares its declared interface against the immediately preceding ACTIVE version and records (without blocking) a `COMPATIBILITY_WARNING` for breaking changes: an input that was optional and is now required, or an output that was previously required and has been removed. Publication is not blocked (SHOULD, not MUST — a major-version bump may be an intentional breaking change). Implementation: `src/repository/process_module_catalog.zig` (publish compares against prior ACTIVE); integration coverage `tests/integration/plc03_compatibility_test.zig` (5/5 PASS).
- **feat(plc): PLC-04 Cross-tenant module distribution RELEASED.** A process module published by one tenant (the "publishing tenant") may be shared to other tenants ("subscribing tenants") via an explicit platform-admin-authorised sharing grant recorded in `process_module_catalog_share`. By default, no tenant's catalog is visible to any other tenant. `module_ref` resolution only considers versions visible to the resolving tenant; an unresolved cross-tenant reference fails via PLC-01's not-found path (no information leak about the publishing tenant). Revocation of a grant does not retroactively terminate running instances — grants govern new activations only. Implementation: `migrations/1161_plc01_process_module_catalog.sql` (share table); `src/repository/process_module_catalog.zig` (grant/revoke + visibility filter); integration coverage `tests/integration/plc04_visibility_test.zig` (8/8 PASS).

## [Unreleased] — 2026-08-14

### feat(prm): Stage 16 batch 2 — PRM-02/03/04/05 RELEASED (WF02-prm-batch2-20260814)

- **feat(prm): PRM-02 Pre-promotion plan validation RELEASED.** The platform validates a promotion plan before any transaction opens. Validation enforces: no concurrent promotions on the same definition, no conflicts in the plan (PRM-05), role-mapping completeness for any SOL-01/02-installed definitions (SOL-03 gate), and that every artefact in the plan is present in the source environment. Validation failures return HTTP 422 listing the specific violation. All 4 ACs implemented: conflict detection, concurrent-promotion detection, role-mapping completeness check, artefact-presence check.
- **feat(prm): PRM-03 Sandbox teardown does not block promotion RELEASED.** Sandbox is released on every exit path of the assertion re-run including assertion failure, infrastructure failure, and panic. A release failure appends `PROMOTION_ASSERTION_TEARDOWN_FAILED` and sets `promotion_assertion_runs.status` to `teardown_failed` but never converts a passing run into a promotion failure. The leaked-sandbox reaper reclaims and records reclamation against the originating run.
- **feat(prm): PRM-04 Promotion rollback by version pointer move RELEASED.** `POST /api/v1/definitions/{process_key}/rollback` re-points the tenant's active version to a previously active version and appends `DEFINITION_VERSION_ROLLED_BACK`. No DDL executes. In-flight instances continue on their PD-08 snapshot. HTTP 422 `VersionNeverActive` for an unknown rollback target; HTTP 403 for non-platform-admin callers; the superseded review row moves to `superseded` with `superseded_by` referencing the rollback event.
- **feat(prm): PRM-05 Three-way conflict detection on plan apply RELEASED.** Plan apply uses a three-way comparison (base/theirs/incoming). Artefacts are classified `unchanged`, `clean_update`, `local_only`, or `conflict`. A `conflict` artefact is blocked until a resolution (`keep_local` / `take_incoming` / `merged`) is recorded with resolving principal and timestamp. Conflicts are surfaced via HTTP 409 before any transaction opens.

Release-decision file: `docs/status/WF02-prm-batch2-20260814-RELEASE-DECISION.yaml` (APPROVED). Requirements PRM-02, PRM-03, PRM-04, PRM-05 advanced from `VALIDATED` to `RELEASED`.

### feat(sol): Stage 15 — Solution pack SOL-01/02/03 RELEASED (WF02-sol-batch1-20260814)

- **feat(sol): SOL-01 Solution pack manifest RELEASED.** The platform exports a "solution pack" — a self-contained JSON document listing one or more process definitions, their transitive `module_ref` dependencies (PLC-01), the service catalog entries (REPO-07) referenced by any SERVICE_TASK in the set, the variable schemas referenced by any variable key in the set, and a flat manifest of every distinct ROLE name found in any HUMAN_TASK `assignee_type = ROLE` node across the set. Each pack carries `pack_id`, `version` (semver), and `bpm_export_schema_version`. Cross-tenant `module_ref` dependencies are inlined into the pack unless the publishing tenant has marked the module non-exportable, in which case export fails with a structured error naming the blocking module. Implementation: `src/solution/pack_builder.zig`, `src/solution/store.zig`, `src/solution/types.zig`, `src/api/routes/solution_packs.zig`; design artefact `src/design/sol-batch1-solution-pack.md`; integration coverage `tests/integration/sol01_export_test.zig` (4/4 PASS); test spec `tests/specs/sol-01-03-solution-pack.md`.
- **feat(sol): SOL-02 Solution pack installation into a target tenant RELEASED.** An authorised caller (`PLATFORM_ADMIN` or `PROCESS_DESIGNER` holding install rights on the target tenant) installs a solution pack into any target tenant by ID. Installation creates all bundled definitions as `DRAFT`, registers or matches all bundled service catalog and variable-schema entries (rejecting on name+schema conflict with HTTP 409; reusing on name+schema match), and returns a role-mapping checklist of unresolved ROLE names. Idempotent on re-install (matching `pack_id`+`version` left untouched, warning returned). New install over a prior version: 1.x definitions are not deleted, new DRAFTs land under PD-03 versioning. Schema: `migrations/1158_sol02_solution_pack_installs.sql`, `migrations/1160_sol_definition_install_fk.sql`; implementation `src/solution/store.zig`, `src/api/routes/solution_packs.zig`; integration coverage `tests/integration/sol02_install_test.zig` (5/5 PASS).
- **feat(sol): SOL-03 Role-mapping completion gate before activation RELEASED.** A definition installed via SOL-02 is not activatable (`DRAFT → ACTIVE`) while any ROLE name in its pack manifest has no binding in the target tenant's IDN-05 role registry. Activation against an incomplete role mapping fails with HTTP 422 listing the unbound roles. Definitions created directly via PD-01 are unaffected (no manifest, no gate). Schema: `migrations/1159_sol03_solution_pack_role_map.sql`; implementation `src/solution/store.zig`, `src/api/routes/solution_packs.zig`; integration coverage `tests/integration/sol03_role_gate_test.zig` (3/3 PASS, MaxRSS 10M stable, no leak diagnostics).
- **Gate results (run WF02-sol-batch1-20260814, squash-merge commit 99ad43db, PR #783).** `zig build` exit 0; full suite 1071/1135 (64 skipped, matches baseline, no new failures); SOL-01 integration 4/4, SOL-02 integration 5/5, SOL-03 integration 3/3 (no leaks); NFR-01 p99 read 0.913 ms (target ≤200), NFR-01 p99 write 2.303 ms (target ≤500), NFR-02 append throughput 13655.925 events/sec (target ≥1000), NFR-04 memory no-leak PASS, NFR-07 audit chain distinct hashes PASS; `python tools/lint_handoffs.py` exit 0. Release-decision file: `docs/status/release-decision-WF02-sol-batch1-20260814.yaml` (APPROVED).
- **Fix history (commit 12972390 on top of branch base, folded into 99ad43db by rebase):**
  - **ISS-SOL01-TC02 — audit chain `digest()` unqualified in `tenant_default` search_path.** `bpm_audit_compute_chain_hash` was called without a schema prefix and silently fell through to a different `digest()` overload inside the per-tenant search_path, producing a 64-zero sentinel that collided on the unique index `uq_audit_entries_tenant_chain_hash`. Fixed by `migrations/1107_fix_audit_chain_text_resource_id.sql` qualifying `public.digest(...)` and re-creating `bpm_audit_chain_canonical_payload` with `TEXT resource_id` so distinct payloads produce distinct hashes.
  - **ISS-SOL02-COMPILE — `defer`/`return` in `sol02_install_test.zig:488` prevented compilation.** Cleanup was restructured so the `defer` block no longer contained `return`; the test-integration-sol02 binary now compiles and passes 5/5.
  - **ISS-SOL03-LEAKS — `installPack` dupe not freed in `src/solution/store.zig:497` (memory leak).** `new_definition_id` is now `allocator.free`'d in the store.zig:497 path; the test-integration-sol03 binary exits 0 with zero leak diagnostics and stable 10M MaxRSS across 3 runs.
- **GH-758 wiring (in the same squash):** the solution-pack code path is now correctly threaded through the post-GH-752/ISS-0692 `pg_advisory_lock` zero-timeout bracket — no integration test hangs; the audit-chain hash correctness (NFR-07) was verified through the SOL-01 export path which itself emits audit entries.
- Requirements SOL-01, SOL-02, SOL-03 advanced from `PENDING` to `RELEASED`. Step-04 TEST-RUNNER initial FAIL (commit 03e1f170) was re-driven green by fix commit 12972390, producing step-04-rework1 PASS (2026-08-14T07:50:57Z) against the post-merge HEAD. ### feat(prm): Stage 16 batch 1 — PRM-06/07/08/09 RELEASED (WF02-prm-batch1-20260814)

- **feat(prm): PRM-06 Pre-promotion assertion re-run RELEASED.** The platform re-runs the assertions carried by the promotion artifact as a pre-production gate in an ephemeral sandbox loaded only with the artifact's `fixtures[]`, running under a frozen clock, RNG seeded from `rng_seed`, and the stub effect recorder. Results are recorded in `promotion_assertion_runs` with `UNIQUE (tenant_id, idempotency_key)`; key pattern `promotion_rerun:<review_id>:<plan_digest>`. All 5 ACs implemented: idempotency on duplicate apply, fixture isolation, deterministic replay after stripping `non_deterministic_fields`, HTTP 422 on assertion failure with failing identifiers, HTTP 503 `SandboxUnavailable` on 60-second sandbox claim timeout.
- **feat(prm): PRM-07 Sandbox teardown does not block promotion RELEASED.** Sandbox is released on every exit path of the assertion re-run including assertion failure, infrastructure failure, and panic. A release failure appends `PROMOTION_ASSERTION_TEARDOWN_FAILED` and sets `promotion_assertion_runs.status` to `teardown_failed` but never converts a passing run into a promotion failure. The leaked-sandbox reaper reclaims and records reclamation against the originating run.
- **feat(prm): PRM-08 Promotion rollback by version pointer move RELEASED.** `POST /api/v1/definitions/{process_key}/rollback` re-points the tenant's active version to a previously active version and appends `DEFINITION_VERSION_ROLLED_BACK`. No DDL executes. In-flight instances continue on their PD-08 snapshot. HTTP 422 `VersionNeverActive` for an unknown rollback target; HTTP 403 for non-platform-admin callers; the superseded review row moves to `superseded` with `superseded_by` referencing the rollback event.
- **feat(prm): PRM-09 Solution pack update conflict resolution RELEASED.** Pack updates use a three-way comparison (base/theirs/incoming). Artefacts are classified `unchanged`, `clean_update`, `local_only`, or `conflict`. A `conflict` artefact is blocked until a resolution (`keep_local` / `take_incoming` / `merged`) is recorded with resolving principal and timestamp. Updates are delivered as a `PRM-01` promotion plan and pass the `PRM-05` gate. `PRM-02` rejects any plan carrying an unresolved conflict before any transaction opens.
- Gate results (run WF02-prm-batch1-20260814, commit 791ab789): build PASS, unit PASS (1071/1135; 64 skipped pre-existing on main), integration PRM-06/07 9/9 PASS, integration PRM-08 4/4 PASS, integration PRM-09 4/4 PASS, lint PASS (0 BLOCKER, 0 MAJOR), frontend typecheck PASS.

### feat(frontend): PW-13/PW-16 Batch 19 — RND-UI-05, RND-UI-06, GRD-UI-06, GRD-UI-07 RELEASED (WF02-pw13-pw16-batch19-20260813)

- **feat(frontend): RND-UI-05 — rate-limit backpressure with retry countdown.** `web/src/components/ui/RateLimitBackpressure.tsx` renders the `rate-limit` renderer state with the `retryAfter` value parsed from the `Retry-After` response header by `web/src/utils/getRetryAfterSeconds.ts`. The countdown ticks at one-second intervals inside an `aria-live="polite"` region and fires `refetch()` exactly once at zero — never a second time without a user action. A 429 without `Retry-After` classifies as `fetch-failure`. Wired through `QueryStateBoundary` and `web/src/api/client.ts` across the 8 data-fetching pages.
- **feat(frontend): RND-UI-06 — three-action write conflict resolution.** A 409 on a write now classifies as `stale-version`, renders `web/src/components/ui/StaleVersionError.tsx`, and mounts `web/src/components/ui/ConflictResolver.tsx` in a portal offering exactly three actions: *Refetch latest*, *Merge manually*, *Discard mine*. The SPA never re-issues the write automatically between the 409 and a user action. *Refetch latest* retains the local draft in `web/src/stores/definitionDraftStore.ts` and surfaces it via `DraftBanner`; *Merge manually* opens `JsonDiffView` (server payload left, local draft right) and posts the merged body at the version carried by `X-Resource-Version`; *Discard mine* routes through `<ConfirmDialog confirmVariant="danger">`.
- **feat(frontend): GRD-UI-06 — accessibility gate per canonical surface.** The `@axe-core/playwright` gate infrastructure landed as `web/tests/a11y/a11yGate.ts`, `a11yConfig.ts`, `reportWriter.ts` and `axe-build-flag.ts`, driven by `web/tests/e2e/a11y-gate.e2e.spec.ts` — one test per canonical surface (Task Inbox, Instance List, Instance Detail, Definition List, Process Designer, Login) against the real backend, no HTTP mocking. `serious`/`critical` violations fail the gate naming rule ID, impact and target selector; `moderate`/`minor` are recorded at severity MINOR and do not block. `color-contrast` remains enabled. **Known scope note:** the on-page axe-core runtime scan is **DEFERRED to the WF-05 UAT run** by design — `loadAxe()` runtime-skips with an explicit message when `@axe-core/playwright` is absent, so the gate is exercised as a dry-run module here and executes for real once Keycloak + BPM API + PostgreSQL are all reachable. The pre-flight `verifyBackendReachable()` probes are unit-level and pass.
- **feat(frontend): GRD-UI-07 — generated per-field ARIA wiring.** `web/src/components/forms/FieldFactory.tsx` now emits, for every generated field, a `<label htmlFor>` bound to the input id, `aria-required` reflecting the JSON Schema `required` list, `aria-describedby` pointing at the field hint node (`ariaHints.ts`), and `aria-invalid` + `aria-errormessage` pointing at the field error node whenever the field is in error. `DynamicFormRenderer` sets `aria-busy` on the form element while a submit is in flight. The invariant "whenever `aria-invalid` is `true`, the node referenced by `aria-errormessage` exists in the DOM" is enforced by `web/tests/a11y/aria-validator.ts`.
- **Release gate (Step 05 RELEASE-VALIDATOR, decision APPROVED):** `pnpm type-check` exit 0, `pnpm lint` exit 0 (`--max-warnings 0` across the whole `web/` tree), `pnpm vitest run tests/unit` 110/110 passing across 19 files, `pnpm build` exit 0 (380 modules, 5.02 s). Frontend bundle-size documented **N/A** — no bundle-size NFR exists in NFR-01..07. No-mock contract honoured: zero `vi.mock` / `page.route` / `jest.mock` / `fetchMock` / MSW invocations across the 8 Batch 19 test files. Decision file: `docs/status/release-decision-WF02-pw13-pw16-batch19-20260813.yaml`. One rework cycle was required (Step 02b-rework1, commit `78d347f0`) to clear 4 `@typescript-eslint/no-unused-vars` errors in the conflict-resolver E2E spec. Requirements RND-UI-05, RND-UI-06, GRD-UI-06 and GRD-UI-07 advanced from DRAFT to RELEASED.

### GH-758 closed as duplicate of GH-752/ISS-0692 — ISS-0698 (RESOLVED)

- **GH-758 closed as duplicate of GH-752/ISS-0692 (fixed by commit 9d06bdf4 on main). No code change required.** Diagnostic run `WF03-GH758-20260813` (ISS-0698) reproduced the documented symptom against the pre-`9d06bdf4` `tests/integration/helpers.zig` state, where `acquireIntegrationLock()` (and the parallel `runMigrations` / `runMigrationsForSchema` sites) bracketed the `pg_advisory_lock` acquire with `lock_timeout='90s'` + an ambient `statement_timeout='60s'`. Under contention, PostgreSQL applies `min(lock_timeout, statement_timeout)` to the blocking acquire — when ≥ 3 binaries queued behind the same `bpm_test_migrations_public` advisory lock, the effective 60 s cap fired `57014 query_canceled` and the resulting error path propagated slowly through `build_runner` IPC, presenting as the perceived "client hang after acquireIntegrationLock" (backend PID `state=idle`, `wait_event=ClientRead`, last successful statement `SET statement_timeout='60s'`). GH-752/ISS-0692 final fix `9d06bdf4` (merged into `main` 2026-08-13 12:21) zeroes both timeouts around every `pg_advisory_lock` acquire, eliminating the cancellation path. Branch base `e9b1294d` is post-`9d06bdf4`; `zig build test-integration-pin01 --verbose` exits 0 with no test-binary stderr on the current branch — symptom no longer reproducible. Closing GH-758 as duplicate of GH-752/ISS-0692; the prior `scratch/gh758_*.log` evidence captured during the de572f01-era bracket state is stale (trace labels read `lock_timeout=90s`, current code reads `0`) and should not be relied on for future diagnosis.

### Scheduler idempotency enforcement restored — GH-766 (RESOLVED)

- **fix(scheduler): GH-766 — appendEventInTx writes to plat_event_idempotency for dedup enforcement.** After PAR-01 partitioning (migration 1147) removed the `uq_event_idempotency` unique index from `events`, `appendEventInTx` in `src/scheduler/scheduler.zig` no longer enforced idempotency for timer-fired events. `TC-SCH-02-03` was failing because the pre-seeded duplicate idempotency key caused no conflict (the constraint was gone). Fix: added a third `conn.exec` in `appendEventInTx` that INSERTs into `plat_event_idempotency(idempotency_key, event_id, created_at)`; duplicate keys now trigger a PRIMARY KEY violation that propagates as `SchedulerError.TransactionFailed` via the ISS-0618 error surfacing. Updated `TC-SCH-02-03` to pre-seed `plat_event_idempotency` instead of relying on the removed `events` constraint.

### Serialize public-schema test binaries — ISS-0696/GH-760 (RESOLVED)

- **fix(tests): GH-760/ISS-0696 — serialize 5 public-schema test binaries to prevent parallel write conflicts.** 5 test binaries (`iss205_test`, `iss203_test`, `iss106_test`, `env01_test`, `sch02_test`) were removed from the parallel pool in `build.zig` and replaced with a serial chain (`run_iss106_serial_public → run_iss203_serial_public → run_iss205_serial_public → run_sch02_serial_public → run_env_serial_public`) that executes after the parallel pool completes. This eliminates the `resetTestData()` cross-binary row deletion race (Failure Mode 1: iss203/iss205/sch02/iss106) and the `public.tenant` non-deterministic row presence issue (Failure Mode 2: env01 TC-ENV-01-03). A new guide section `docs/guides/test_developer_guide.md §10.3` documents the serial-chain rule for future contributors. Commits `1e4f31f1` and `0e27759e` on `feature/WF03-GH760-20260813`. Note: 5 pre-existing deterministic failures were discovered in these binaries (separate from the GH-760 parallel conflict — they occur even in isolation): `iss106` PgError.ServerError in full_contract_row_round_trips, `iss203` QueryFailed in TC-ISS-203-02 ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING idempotency key insert, `iss205` PoolError.QueryFailed in TC1/TC2/TC3 webhook outbox, `sch02` TC-SCH-02-03 expected TransactionFailed but got fired=1, `env01` TC-ENV-01-03 wrong tenant_type in production rows. These are forwarded to the global queue for separate WF-03 investigation.

### pg_advisory_lock contention fix — ISS-0692/GH-752 (RESOLVED)

- **fix(tests): unbounded pg_advisory_lock acquire — lock_timeout=0, statement_timeout=0 brackets.** All 4 `pg_advisory_lock` call sites in `tests/integration/helpers.zig` (`runMigrations`, `runMigrationsForSchema` ×2, `TestHarness.init`) now execute `SET lock_timeout=0` and `SET statement_timeout=0` immediately before the blocking acquire, restoring both to their prior values after the lock is held. Root cause: PostgreSQL applies `min(lock_timeout, statement_timeout)` to every statement including blocking advisory lock waits; with `lock_timeout='90s'` and `statement_timeout='60s'` both in scope the effective cap was 60 s — insufficient when ≥ 3 binaries queued concurrently under `-j8`. Fix verified: SE=0 across seeds `0x3236dff` and `0x939cd4a9` in the 5-run acceptance gate (was 37–118 SEs before). `scripts/run-test-integration.{ps1,sh}` default `-j` cap lowered `8→4`; migration `pool_size` raised `2→4`. Commit `91a71ef6` on `feature/WF03-GH752-20260812`. Two pre-existing issues revealed during the gate (TC-RG-01/02 snapshot drift; concurrent public-schema isolation failures) filed as separate GH issues — not caused by this fix.
